Install top shaft collar - LIGHTLY clamp (retention only)\n\n==========================================\nTEST PROTOCOL\n==========================================\nTest 1: Rotation - smooth, slight resistance OK\nTest 2: Axial play - pull shaft, ZERO movement allowed\nTest 3: Drag - if too sticky, spacer too short (lengthen 0.2-0.3mm)\nTest 4: Click - if click on rotation, spacer too long (shorten 0.2-0.3mm)\nTest 5: Dial indicator - shaft runout <0.2mm TIR at shaft end\n\n==========================================\nFORCE PATH (V2.5)\n==========================================\nTop block (rigid)\n -> top bearing inner race\n   -> spacer tube (22.9mm rigid)\n     -> bottom radial bearing inner race\n       -> spacer washer (McMaster 19x28x1.6)\n         -> CM25-L1 spring (compressed 3.20mm = ~35N)\n           -> upper washers\n             -> thrust bearing (preloaded)\n               -> lower washers\n                 -> PETG floor (rigid)\n\nResult: preload set by SPRING COMPRESSION, controlled by spacer length.\n\n==========================================\nPRINT SETTINGS\n==========================================\nBlocks: PETG, 0.2mm layer, 5 perimeters, 40-50% Gyroid infill\n        Pocket opening UP, no supports\nSpacer tube: PETG, 0.1mm layer, 8 perimeters, 100% infill\n             Vertical orientation, slow speed (30mm/s)\nEOF\n\n# Create BOM README\ncat > csm_v25_delivery/3D-Project/04_PURCHASING/README_BOM_V9.txt << 'EOF'\nCSM V3 - BOM V9 (Updated for V2.5)\n====================================\nFile: BOM_V9.html\nDate: April 2026\n\nWHAT CHANGED FROM PREVIOUS BOM:\n\n1. LATCH PIVOT DIAMETER (Item 3 - the small rivet pin)\n   Location: The pin connecting the latch to the hook\n   Tool: Caliper - measure the pin diameter\n   Current design assumes: 1.2-1.5mm\n   Range to expect: 1.0-1.8mm\n   Why it matters: Latch swings on this; affects internal pocket geometry\n\n5. HOOK TIP TO LATCH PIVOT DISTANCE (Items 1 + 3)\n   Location: Horizontal distance from hook tip to the latch rivet\n   Tool: Caliper or ruler - measure the distance along the hook curve\n   Current design assumes: ~8-10mm\n   Range to expect: 7-12mm\n   Why it matters: Determines latch swingarm length and hook catch geometry\n\nEXTRA (helpful but not blocking):\n\n6. HOOK TIP RADIUS (Item 1 - the curved hook part)\n   Location: The hook opening radius\n   Tool: Measure across the hook opening width\n   Current design assumes: ~1-1.5mm\n   Why helpful: Affects yarn catch smoothness, not blocking\n\n7.         "text": "Important Parts of the Legare \u00b7 Most Legare machines came with a 54 slot cylinder and a 72 slot cylinder\n\nThe 54 cylinder with the 36 ribber makes a nice 2/1 rib (other combinations are possible, but not 1/1) The 72/36 combination can do a 1/1 rib, along with a number of other options. Many types of socks can be knit from the standard combination of cylinders and ribber. ... The machine\u2019s ability to produce a wide variety of stitch length (small to large) is based on the depth of the v- cam inside the cam shell. Some machines have a deep v cam \u2013 and can do large stitches . There are some short \u00b7 v-cams that can only do smaller stitches. Most machines are versatile enough to do a variety of stitch lengths \u00b7 The height of the yarn carrier is crucial to the machine knitting properly. ... The ribber height should be set high enough that the web of the knitting can easily pass through between the \u00b7 ribber and the cylinder. But it should not be set much higher.\n\nThe ribber switch should be on the outside position (toward the cylinder) when out of work (not knitting the ... The ribber tension toward the inside makes a larger stitch, toward the outside \u2013 a tighter stitch. Most times \u00b7 you should adjust the tension of the sock by adjusting the cylinder tension only. The ribber will only operate in the forward position. Do not remove the ribber drive pin when ribbing - unless you are working on heels, and have taken the cylinder ...\n\nThe ribber dial adjuster screw is used to line up the ribber dial slot with a cylinder slot.",

        "type": "knowledge",
        "title": "Ask The Bellwether: What difference does the cylinder size make?",
        "url": "https://askthebellwether.blogspot.com/2008/01/what-difference-does-cylinder-size-make.html",
        "metadata": {
          "type": "webpage_metadata",
          "site_domain": "blogspot.com",
          "favicon_url": "https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?sz=64&domain=blogspot.com",
          "site_name": "Blogger"
        },
        "is_missing": false,
        "text": "There are 120, 100, 96, 84, 80, 72, 60, 54, and 48 slot cylinders ... the 80, 72, 60, and 54 are the most common; 120 is really rare! The cylinder sits inside the cam, and the needles ride up and down the slots of the cylinder to knit the socks.\n\nThe main thing about them is the number of slots -- we say \"this is a 72\", meaning the cylinder has 72 slots in it. The one shown here is a 72. There are 120, 100, 96, 84, 80, 72, 60, 54, and 48 slot cylinders ... the 80, 72, 60, and 54 are the most common; 120 is really rare! The cylinder sits inside the cam, and the needles ride up and down the slots of the cylinder to knit the socks. A bit like a geared, extra-slotted, knitting mushroom. For dimensions, most cylinders have a standard diameter (with small variances) of 4 1/2\u201d, and compound cylinders (rarer) have a working diameter of 3 1/2\u201d. Most Legare 400 and Autoknitter cylinders are interchangeable. The Legare 47 tends to have a slightly smaller cylinder size. Though, older machines can tend to fit only the cylinders that come with them, due to shifting in roundness or size or the hand-machining of them in the first place.\n\nSo it's best to take very close measurements if you buy a cylinder blind, unless the seller is a knowledgeable sock machine person themselves with the same machine brand and model (and series! I had 3 Legare 400's once obviously from different generations -- I was lucky though, they all swapped cylinders smoothly.) The number of slots controls the sock size (or, rather, number of needles in the slots). A 72 or 80 slot cylinder uses sock yarn like regia and opal to knit nice adult socks for pretty much any adult \u2013 adjust the tension looser for mr. giant feet, tighter for ms. normal foot. A 60 slot or 54 slot can take a slightly thicker yarn (I\u2019ve only tried up to DK weight \u2013 the needles tend to split thicker yarns, a real pain!) to knit an adult sock, or stick with sock yarn and a tight tension to knit kids\u2019 socks. The smallest sock I can knit on a 54 at the tightest tension it will reasonably knit fit my daughter\u2019s size 13 (she\u2019s 8 years old, so that\u2019s a kids 13) feet.\n\nThe compound cylinders are interesting; at the base they are 4 1/2\" in diameter, but they neck-down to 3 1/2\" at the top, and have needles with a very long tail-leg so the lifters in the cam can raise the needles. They are useful for toddler and baby socks, and really skinny scarves. There are also 100 and more slot cylinders, those are useful for very, very fine yarns. I have knit a tube on my 100, but that\u2019s all so far. there\u2019s a cone of zephyr yarn (merino/silk laceweight) in my stash waiting for it \u2026 someday! As for ribbers ... well, that's another post! *** In addition to my continued all-time favorite sock-machine eye-candy blog, soxophoneplayer, and the yahoo lists for sock machines, there's now a fun circular sock machine group on ravelry! Join ravelry, and join the fun ... share projects, photos, yarns, and stories. See the list of posts on sock machines for more.",

If we use them in a 90mm cylinder:\n- Butts may not reach the cam properly\n- Sock circumference will be wrong (too small)\n- Cam-needle interaction geometry breaks\n\n## Three Options\n\n### Option A: Redesign for Legare standard (RECOMMENDED)\n- Cylinder OD: 90mm \u2192 **114.3mm**\n- Slot count: 100 \u2192 **72** (most common Legare standard)\n- Sinker ring: 110mm \u2192 ~135mm\n- Cam ring: 140mm \u2192 ~165mm\n- Cassette base: 150mm \u2192 ~175mm\n- **Significant rework** but proper compatibility\n- **Frame (420\u00d7320) probably still works**\n- Drive system / bearings / motor unchanged\n\n### Option B: Keep 90mm, return needles, order different ones\n- Need **smaller needles** (~40-45mm long, smaller butt)\n- These exist for some 3D-printed CSMs (smaller scale)\n- Lose $56 on the needle pack (return-restricted)\n- Keep all current design work\n\n### Option C: Hybrid \u2014 Smaller scale machine\n- Use 90mm + these long needles anyway\n- Will probably work but produce **smaller-than-standard socks**\n- Cam geometry needs verification\n- Risk of failure\n\n## My Recommendation\n\n**Option A** is the right path because:\n1. Your needles are already correct standard parts\n2. The CSM ecosystem is built around 4.5\" cylinders\n3. You can use commercially available cam patterns/references\n4. Future replacement parts are widely available\n5. Drive system/bearings/motor design all stay the same\n\n## Questions for You\n\nBefore I do major rework, I need to confirm:\n\n1. **What's your project goal?** Build a working sock machine for actual socks, or experimental small-scale machine?\n\n2. **Why was 90mm chosen originally?** Was it based on something specific, or just an assumption?\n\n3. **Do you want to proceed with Option A (redesign for 4.5\" / 114.3mm)?**\n\nThis is a critical decision point. **I'd rather pause now than build something that doesn't work with your actual needles.**\n\nWhat's your preference?",

=============================================================================\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\"CSM_Cylinder_V1_0\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# HELPER: Build a single slot solid\n# =============================================================================\n# Slot is a rectangular cut from outer surface, going RADIALLY inward.\n# Slot occupies the TOP zone of the cylinder (stem zone).\n# Below the slot, a butt cavity is cut (wider/shorter, axially).\n# Slot is positioned by angular index (0 to 71) and the BOTTOM-OF-SLOT Z height.\n\ndef build_slot_cutter(angle_deg, slot_width, slot_depth, slot_length,\n                      butt_width, butt_height, butt_depth,\n                      cyl_height, slot_chamfer):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build a cutter shape representing one slot + butt cavity at the given angle.\n    Returns a single Part shape ready to be cut from the cylinder.\n    \n    Geometry: oriented along +X axis initially, then rotated to angle_deg.\n    The cutter extends radially OUTWARD from cylinder center.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Slot top is at Z = cyl_height (top face)\n    # Slot bottom is at Z = cyl_height - slot_length\n    # Butt cavity below slot: Z = (cyl_height - slot_length) down to (cyl_height - slot_length - butt_height)\n    \n    slot_top_z    = cyl_height\n    slot_bot_z    = cyl_height - slot_length\n    butt_top_z    = slot_bot_z\n    butt_bot_z    = slot_bot_z - butt_height\n    \n    # Slot shape: thin rectangular box\n    # Place it so it cuts INTO the cylinder from outside\n    # Box dimensions: width (Y), depth (X going inward), length (Z)\n    # Position: centered at cylinder OD on +X axis, Y centered, Z spanning slot length\n    \n    # Slot box - extends from outer surface inward by slot_depth + safety margin\n    slot_x_outer = CYL_OUTER_R + 1.0   # 1mm beyond OD for clean cut\n    slot_x_inner = CYL_OUTER_R - slot_depth\n    slot_box = Part.makeBox(\n        slot_x_outer - slot_x_inner,  # X dimension (radial)\n        slot_width,                   # Y dimension (circumferential)\n        slot_length                   # Z dimension (axial)\n    )\n    # Box is created at origin, so translate to place it correctly\n    slot_box.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(slot_x_inner, -slot_width/2.0, slot_bot_z))\n    \n    # Butt cavity box - wider and taller than slot\n    butt_x_outer = CYL_OUTER_R + 1.0\n    butt_x_inner = CYL_OUTER_R - butt_depth\n    butt_box = Part.makeBox(\n        butt_x_outer - butt_x_inner,\n        butt_width,\n        butt_height\n    )\n    butt_box.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(butt_x_inner, -butt_width/2.0, butt_bot_z))\n    \n    # Lead-in chamfer at slot top (a small angled wedge above the slot opening)\n    # This is a small triangular prism at the slot's top edge\n    # Approximated by a slightly enlarged slot-top region\n    if slot_chamfer > 0.0:\n        cham_x_outer = CYL_OUTER_R + 1.0\n        cham_x_inner = CYL_OUTER_R - slot_chamfer  # shallow chamfer\n        cham_box = Part.makeBox(\n            cham_x_outer - cham_x_inner,\n            slot_width + 2 * slot_chamfer,    # wider at top\n            slot_chamfer + 0.1                # short chamfer height\n        )\n        cham_box.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(cham_x_inner,\n                                          -(slot_width + 2 * slot_chamfer) / 2.0,\n                                          slot_top_z - slot_chamfer))\n        cutter = slot_box.fuse(butt_box).fuse(cham_box)\n    else:\n        cutter = slot_box.fuse(butt_box)\n    \n    # Rotate cutter to the requested angle around Z axis\n    cutter.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\n                  FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                  angle_deg)\n    \n    return cutter\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD: FULL 72-SLOT CYLINDER\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n[1/2] Building FULL 72-slot cylinder...\")\n\n# Base cylinder shell (hollow outer_cyl.cut(inner_cyl)\n\n# Hub pocket on bottom face\npocket = Part.makeCylinder(HUB_POCKET_D / 2.0, HUB_POCKET_H + 0.1)\npocket.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.05))\ncyl_body = cyl_body.cut(pocket)\n\n# Hub pocket lead-in chamfer\nif HUB_POCKET_CHAM > 0:\n    cham_outer = Part.makeCone(\n        HUB_POCKET_D / 2.0 + HUB_POCKET_CHAM,\n        HUB_POCKET_D / 2.0,\n        HUB_POCKET_CHAM,\n    )\n    cham_outer.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.001))\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(cham_outer)\n\n# Bolt holes (4x M5 with counterbores)\nfor i in range(BOLT_COUNT):\n    angle = i * (360.0 / BOLT_COUNT)\n    rad = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\n    x = rad * math.cos(math.radians(angle))\n    y = rad * math.sin(math.radians(angle))\n    \n    # M5 clearance hole through full height\n    hole = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_HOLE_D / 2.0, CYL_HEIGHT + 2)\n    hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(hole)\n    \n    # Counterbore on bottom (M5 socket head)\n    cb = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_CB_D / 2.0, BOLT_CB_H + 0.1)\n    cb.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -0.05))\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(cb)\n\n# 72 needle slots + butt cavities\nprint(\"  Cutting 72 slots...\")\nfor i in range(SLOT_COUNT):\n    angle = i * SLOT_PITCH_DEG\n    cutter = build_slot_cutter(\n        angle, SLOT_WIDTH, SLOT_DEPTH, SLOT_LENGTH,\n        BUTT_WIDTH, BUTT_HEIGHT, BUTT_DEPTH,\n        CYL_HEIGHT, SLOT_CHAMFER\n    )\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(cutter)\n\n# Outer edge chamfers (top and bottom)\nif EDGE_CHAMFER > 0:\n    try:\n        edges_to_chamfer = []\n        bb = cyl_body.BoundBox\n        for e in cyl_body.Edges:\n            v1 = e.Vertexes[0].Point\n            v2 = e.Vertexes[1].Point\n            # Find horizontal circular edges at outer radius\n            if abs(v1.z - v2.z) < 0.01:  # horizontal edge\n                r1 = (v1.x ** 2 + v1.y ** 2) ** 0.5\n                # Only outermost edge (at OD), top and bottom faces only\n                if abs(r1 - CYL_OUTER_R) < 0.05:\n                    if abs(v1.z - 0) < 0.05 or abs(v1.z - CYL_HEIGHT) < 0.05:\n                        edges_to_chamfer.append(e)\n        if edges_to_chamfer:\n            cyl_body = cyl_body.makeChamfer(EDGE_CHAMFER, edges_to_chamfer)\n            print(f\"  Chamfered {len(edges_to_chamfer)} outer edges\")\n    except Exception as ex:\n        print(f\"  Outer chamfer skipped: {ex}\")\n\nobj_full = doc.addObject(\"Part::Feature\", \"Cylinder_V1_0_FULL\")\nobj_full.Shape = cyl_body\nobj_full.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.3, 0.5,                   FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1),\n                                FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\n                                WEDGE_ANGLE_DEG)\n# This makes a partial cylinder spanning 0 to WEDGE_ANGLE_DEG degrees\n# Intersect with our hollow ring to get just the wedge\nwedge_body = wedge_body.common(keep_sector)\n\n# Cut 6 slots at angles 2.5, 7.5, 12.5, 17.5, 22.5, 27.5 deg (centered SLOT_CHAMFER\n    )\n    wedge_body = wedge_body.cut(cutter)\n\n# Position wedge offset from full cylinder for visualization\nwedge_body.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(150, 0, 0))\n\nobj_wedge =          {CYL_HEIGHT}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Slot count:         {SLOT_COUNT}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Slot pitch:         {SLOT_PITCH_DEG}deg / {SLOT_PITCH_MM:.3f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Slot W x D:         {SLOT_WIDTH} x {SLOT_DEPTH}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Slot length:        {SLOT_LENGTH}mm axial\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Wall between slots: {WALL_BETWEEN:.3f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Butt cavity:        {BUTT_WIDTH} x {BUTT_HEIGHT}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Hub pocket:         D{HUB_POCKET_D} x {HUB_POCKET_H}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Bolt pattern:       {BOLT_COUNT}x M5 @ PCD =============================================================================\\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\\\"CSM_Cylinder_V1_0\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# HELPER: Build a single slot solid\\n# =============================================================================\\n# Slot is a rectangular cut from outer surface, going RADIALLY inward.\\n# Slot occupies the TOP zone of the cylinder (stem zone).\\n# Below the slot, a butt cavity is cut (wider/shorter, axially).\\n# Slot is positioned by angular index (0 to 71) and the BOTTOM-OF-SLOT Z height.\\n\\ndef build_slot_cutter(angle_deg, slot_width, slot_depth, slot_length,\\n                      butt_width, butt_height, butt_depth,\\n                      cyl_height, slot_chamfer):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Build a cutter shape representing one slot + butt cavity at the given angle.\\n    Returns a single Part shape ready to be cut from the cylinder.\\n    \\n    Geometry: oriented along +X axis initially, then rotated to angle_deg.\\n    The cutter extends radially OUTWARD from cylinder center.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Slot top is at Z = cyl_height (top face)\\n    # Slot bottom is at Z = cyl_height - slot_length\\n    # Butt cavity below slot: Z = (cyl_height - slot_length) down to (cyl_height - slot_length - butt_height)\\n    \\n    slot_top_z    = cyl_height\\n    slot_bot_z    = cyl_height - slot_length\\n    butt_top_z    = slot_bot_z\\n    butt_bot_z    = slot_bot_z - butt_height\\n    \\n    # Slot shape: thin rectangular box\\n    # Place it so it cuts INTO the cylinder from outside\\n    # Box dimensions: width (Y), depth (X going inward), length (Z)\\n    # Position: centered at cylinder OD on +X axis, Y centered, Z spanning slot length\\n    \\n    # Slot box - extends from outer surface inward by slot_depth + safety margin\\n    slot_x_outer = CYL_OUTER_R + 1.0   # 1mm beyond OD for clean cut\\n    slot_x_inner = CYL_OUTER_R - slot_depth\\n    slot_box = Part.makeBox(\\n        slot_x_outer - slot_x_inner,  # X dimension (radial)\\n        slot_width,                   # Y dimension (circumferential)\\n        slot_length                   # Z dimension (axial)\\n    )\\n    # Box is created at origin, so translate to place it correctly\\n    slot_box.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(slot_x_inner, -slot_width/2.0, slot_bot_z))\\n    \\n    # Butt cavity box - wider and taller than slot\\n    butt_x_outer = CYL_OUTER_R + 1.0\\n    butt_x_inner = CYL_OUTER_R - butt_depth\\n    butt_box = Part.makeBox(\\n        butt_x_outer - butt_x_inner,\\n        butt_width,\\n        butt_height\\n    )\\n    butt_box.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(butt_x_inner, -butt_width/2.0, butt_bot_z))\\n    \\n    # Lead-in chamfer at slot top (a small angled wedge above the slot opening)\\n    # This is a small triangular prism at the slot's top edge\\n    # Approximated by a slightly enlarged slot-top region\\n    if slot_chamfer > 0.0:\\n        cham_x_outer = CYL_OUTER_R + 1.0\\n        cham_x_inner = CYL_OUTER_R - slot_chamfer  # shallow chamfer\\n        cham_box = Part.makeBox(\\n            cham_x_outer - cham_x_inner,\\n            slot_width + 2 * slot_chamfer,    # wider at top\\n            slot_chamfer + 0.1                # short chamfer height\\n        )\\n        cham_box.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(cham_x_inner,\\n                                          -(slot_width + 2 * slot_chamfer) / 2.0,\\n                                          slot_top_z - slot_chamfer))\\n        cutter = slot_box.fuse(butt_box).fuse(cham_box)\\n    else:\\n        cutter = slot_box.fuse(butt_box)\\n    \\n    # Rotate cutter to the requested angle around Z axis\\n    cutter.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0),\\n                  FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                  angle_deg)\\n    \\n    return cutter\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD: FULL 72-SLOT CYLINDER\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"\\\\n[1/2] Building FULL 72-slot cylinder...\\\")\\n\\n# Base cylinder shell (hollow tube)\\nouter_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(CYL_OUTER_R, CYL_HEIGHT)\\ninner_cyl = Part.makeCylinder(CYL_INNER_R, CYL_HEIGHT + 2)\\ninner_cyl.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncyl_body = outer_cyl.cut(inner_cyl)\\n\\n# Hub pocket on bottom face\\npocket = Part.makeCylinder(HUB_POCKET_D / 2.0, HUB_POCKET_H + 0.1)\\npocket.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.05))\\ncyl_body = cyl_body.cut(pocket)\\n\\n# Hub pocket lead-in chamfer\\nif HUB_POCKET_CHAM > 0:\\n    cham_outer = Part.makeCone(\\n        HUB_POCKET_D / 2.0 + HUB_POCKET_CHAM,\\n        HUB_POCKET_D / 2.0,\\n        HUB_POCKET_CHAM,\\n    )\\n    cham_outer.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -0.001))\\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(cham_outer)\\n\\n# Bolt holes (4x M5 with counterbores)\\nfor i in range(BOLT_COUNT):\\n    angle = i * (360.0 / BOLT_COUNT)\\n    rad = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\\n    x = rad * math.cos(math.radians(angle))\\n    y = rad * math.sin(math.radians(angle))\\n    \\n    # M5 clearance hole through full height\\n    hole = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_HOLE_D / 2.0, CYL_HEIGHT + 2)\\n    hole.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -1))\\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(hole)\\n    \\n    # Counterbore on bottom (M5 socket head)\\n    cb = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_CB_D / 2.0, BOLT_CB_H + 0.1)\\n    cb.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, -0.05))\\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(cb)\\n\\n# 72 needle slots + butt cavities\\nprint(\\\"  Cutting 72 slots...\\\")\\nfor i in range(SLOT_COUNT):\\n    angle = i * SLOT_PITCH_DEG\\n    cutter = build_slot_cutter(\\n        angle, SLOT_WIDTH, SLOT_DEPTH, SLOT_LENGTH,\\n        BUTT_WIDTH, BUTT_HEIGHT, BUTT_DEPTH,\\n        CYL_HEIGHT, SLOT_CHAMFER\\n    )\\n    cyl_body = cyl_body.cut(cutter)\\n\\n# Outer edge chamfers (top and bottom)\\nif EDGE_CHAMFER > 0:\\n    try:\\n        edges_to_chamfer = []\\n        bb = cyl_body.BoundBox\\n        for e in cyl_body.Edges:\\n            v1 = e.Vertexes[0].Point\\n            v2 = e.Vertexes[1].Point\\n            # Find horizontal circular edges at outer radius\\n            if abs(v1.z - v2.z) < 0.01:  # horizontal edge\\n                r1 = (v1.x ** 2 + v1.y ** 2) ** 0.5\\n                # Only outermost edge (at OD), top and bottom faces only\\n                if abs(r1 - CYL_OUTER_R) < 0.05:\\n                    if abs(v1.z - 0) < 0.05 or abs(v1.z - CYL_HEIGHT) < 0.05:\\n                        edges_to_chamfer.append(e)\\n        if edges_to_chamfer:\\n            cyl_body = cyl_body.makeChamfer(EDGE_CHAMFER, edges_to_chamfer)\\n            print(f\\\"  Chamfered {len(edges_to_chamfer)} outer edges\\\")\\n    except Exception as ex:\\n        print(f\\\"  Outer chamfer skipped: {ex}\\\")\\n\\nobj_full = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"Cylinder_V1_0_FULL\\\")\\nobj_full.Shape = cyl_body\\nobj_full.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.3, 0.5, 0.7)\\nprint(f\\\"  Volume:  {cyl_body.Volume:.0f}mm^3\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Surface: {cyl_body.Area:.0f}mm^2\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD: 30 DEG TEST WEDGE\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"\\\\n[2/2] Building 30 deg test wedge (6 slots, 3 width variants)...\\\")\\n\\n# Wedge is a 30-degree pie slice of the cylinder, with 6 slots\\n# Slot widths: [1.20, 1.20, 1.22, 1.22, 1.25, 1.25] for empirical fit testing\\n\\n# Build full ring first\\nwedge_outer = Part.makeCylinder(CYL_OUTER_R, CYL_HEIGHT)\\nwedge_inner = Part.makeCylinder(CYL_INNER_R, CYL_HEIGHT + 2)\\nwedge_inner.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\nwedge_body = wedge_outer.cut(wedge_inner)\\n\\n# Cut to 30-degree wedge: keep angles 0 to 30 deg\\n# Build a \\\"keeping\\\" volume = 30deg sector + a bit of cylinder around\\n# Easiest: subtract a 330-degree sector from the ring\\n\\n# Build a large pie slice covering 330 degrees (the part to remove)\\n# We want to KEEP angles 0 to 30 degrees centered on slots\\n# So slots should be at 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30 deg... actually let's center the wedge\\n# Wedge spans -2.5 to +27.5 deg, slots at 0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25 deg\\n\\n# Simpler: build a sector to KEEP (a 30-deg pie slice) and intersect with the ring\\nkeep_sector = Part.makeCylinder(CYL_OUTER_R + 5, CYL_HEIGHT + 2,\\n                                FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1),\\n                                FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1),\\n                                WEDGE_ANGLE_DEG)\\n# This makes a partial cylinder spanning 0 to WEDGE_ANGLE_DEG degrees\\n# Intersect with our hollow ring to get just the wedge\\nwedge_body = wedge_body.common(keep_sector)\\n\\n# Cut 6 slots at angles 2.5, 7.5, 12.5, 17.5, 22.5, 27.5 deg (centered in wedge slots)\\n# Each with its own width from WEDGE_SLOT_WIDTHS\\nprint(\\\"  Cutting 6 test slots with widths:\\\", WEDGE_SLOT_WIDTHS)\\nfor i, slot_w in enumerate(WEDGE_SLOT_WIDTHS):\\n    angle = (i + 0.5) * SLOT_PITCH_DEG  # centers at 2.5, 7.5, etc.\\n    cutter = build_slot_cutter(\\n        angle, slot_w, SLOT_DEPTH, SLOT_LENGTH,\\n        BUTT_WIDTH, BUTT_HEIGHT, BUTT_DEPTH,\\n        CYL_HEIGHT, SLOT_CHAMFER\\n    )\\n    wedge_body = wedge_body.cut(cutter)\\n\\n# Position wedge offset from full cylinder for visualization\\nwedge_body.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(150, 0, 0))\\n\\nobj_wedge = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"Cylinder_V1_0_TESTWEDGE\\\")\\nobj_wedge.Shape = wedge_body\\nobj_wedge.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.7, 0.5, 0.3)\\nprint(f\\\"  Volume:  {wedge_body.Volume:.0f}mm^3 (~{wedge_body.Volume * 1.27 / 1000:.1f}g PETG)\\\")\\n\\ndoc.recompute()\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# EXPORT STLs\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nExporting STL files...\\\")\\n\\ntry:\\n    # Full cylinder STL (centered at origin)\\n    full_export = obj_full.Shape.copy()\\n    tmp_full = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"tmp_full\\\")\\n    tmp_full.Shape = full_export\\n    Part.export([tmp_full], PATH_STL_FULL)\\n    doc.removeObject(\\\"tmp_full\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  [OK] FULL STL:  {PATH_STL_FULL}\\\")\\n    \\n    # Test wedge STL (translate back to origin for printing)\\n    wedge_export = obj_wedge.Shape.copy()\\n    wedge_export.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-150, 0, 0))  # cancel +150 offset\\n    tmp_wedge = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"tmp_wedge\\\")\\n    tmp_wedge.Shape = wedge_export\\n    Part.export([tmp_wedge], PATH_STL_WEDGE)\\n    doc.removeObject(\\\"tmp_wedge\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  [OK] WEDGE STL: {PATH_STL_WEDGE}\\\")\\nexcept Exception as ex:\\n    print(f\\\"  STL export error: {ex}\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# EXPORT BLUEPRINT SVG (top view + side section)\\n#
